# Agents · R · part 9

982 entries, regular-user to release-readiness. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **regular-user**: Use this agent to have an ordinary, NON-TECHNICAL person actually try a Vivreal screen and say, in plain words, what happened and where they got stuck. Typical triggers include "can a normal person use this", "test this like a real customer", "would a baker figure this out", "try to build a product with variants" and "walk the filters screen as a regular user". It DRIVES the real screen with Playwright, in the installed app first, and reports first-person: what it tried, what it expected, what it got, and where it gave up. HARD RULE: it never reads source code to work out what a control does,… ([HillBombCreations/vivreal-skills](https://github.com/HillBombCreations/vivreal-skills/blob/HEAD/vivreal-product/agents/regular-user.md))
- **regulated-experiment-auditor**: Independent, adversarial auditor of experiment design, implementation, analysis, and decision quality in regulated or high-trust settings. Use before launch (design audit), in-flight (SRM and telemetry health), at readout (validity and Twyman's-law checks), and pre-decision (does the recommendation follow from pre-registered evidence). Trigger proactively whenever the user shares a test plan, dashboard, or readout. Read-only: it surfaces findings and remediations, it does not modify artifacts or run the analysis. Escalate regulatory/compliance concerns to regulated-risk-reviewer. ([weisberg/agile_agentic_analytics](https://github.com/weisberg/agile_agentic_analytics/blob/HEAD/plugins/experimentation/agents/regulated-experiment-auditor.md) · ★1)

Run ONLY when the user explicitly asks - "critique the release", "review the release with several models", "is this release ready", "harden the release", "find what's wrong before we ship".
